tool_result: 1	"""R-TWIN-TEST / NIGHT BUNDLE 4 item 6 — the TWIN-RUN HARNESS (code only; the actual run waits for a
2	memory-safe window after 15:45).
3	
4	It replays ONE recorded market day through the real live loop in a REHEARSAL context that provably cannot touch
5	the live ledgers, and compares the result trade-for-trade with the exam engine over the same recording.
6	
7	TWO independent safety layers, so a bug in either still fails closed:
8	  1. PREVENTIVE — the rehearsal routes every order/ledger write to a throwaway temp dir, never a live path.
9	  2. DETECTIVE  — every live ledger is sha256-hashed BEFORE and AFTER; any change is a FAILED-SAFETY verdict
10	                  (louder than a mismatch). A recorded day that never touches a live file is the pass.
11	
12	The live-loop and exam runners are INJECTED (`live_loop_fn`, `exam_fn`) so this module is unit-tested with fakes
13	and spends nothing; the real runners are supplied at run time after 15:45 (they import the live loop + exam and
14	feed the recorded day — that wiring is the run, deliberately not invoked here).
15	"""
16	from __future__ import annotations
17	
18	import glob
19	import hashlib
20	import os
21	from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
22	
23	_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
24	IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
25	REPORTS = os.path.join(_REPO, "docs", "reports")
26	
27	# Every live ledger that a rehearsal must leave byte-for-byte unchanged.
28	def live_ledgers():
29	    paths = glob.glob(os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "*LEDGER*.csv")) + \
30	        glob.glob(os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "books", "*.csv"))
31	    return sorted(paths)
32	
33	
34	def hash_ledgers(paths=None):
35	    """{path: sha256 or None if missing}. Never raises."""
36	    out = {}
37	    for p in (paths if paths is not None else live_ledgers()):
38	        try:
39	            out[p] = hashlib.sha256(open(p, "rb").read()).hexdigest()
40	        except Exception:
41	            out[p] = None
42	    return out
43	
44	
45	def changed(before, after):
46	    """The ledger paths whose hash changed between two snapshots (a created or deleted file counts)."""
47	    keys = set(before) | set(after)
48	    return sorted(k for k in keys if before.get(k) != after.get(k))
49	
50	
51	# ---- trade-for-trade comparison (the exam is the reference; a difference in the live loop is the defect) ----
52	_FIELDS = ("signal_time", "verdict", "reason", "fill_price", "exit_time", "exit_reason", "pnl_R")
53	
54	
55	def _key(t):
56	    return (t.get("signal_time"), t.get("stock") or t.get("instrument"))
57	
58	
59	def compare(live_trades, exam_trades):
60	    """MATCH, or a numbered list of every difference (which side is wrong). Aligns on (signal_time, instrument)."""
61	    live = {_key(t): t for t in (live_trades or [])}
62	    exam = {_key(t): t for t in (exam_trades or [])}
63	    diffs = []
64	    for k in sorted(set(live) | set(exam), key=lambda x: tuple("" if v is None else str(v) for v in x)):
65	        lt, et = live.get(k), exam.get(k)
66	        if lt is None:
67	            diffs.append("%s %s: in EXAM only (live loop produced no trade)" % k)
68	            continue
69	        if et is None:
70	            diffs.append("%s %s: in LIVE only (exam produced no trade)" % k)
71	            continue
72	        for f in _FIELDS:
73	            if lt.get(f) != et.get(f):
74	                diffs.append("%s %s: %s live=%r exam=%r" % (k[0], k[1], f, lt.get(f), et.get(f)))
75	    verdict = "MATCH" if not diffs else "DIFF"
76	    return {"verdict": verdict, "differences": [("%d. %s" % (i + 1, d)) for i, d in enumerate(diffs)]}
77	
78	
79	def run(recorded_day, live_loop_fn, exam_fn, out=None):
80	    """Replay one recorded day. `live_loop_fn(recorded_day, rehearsal_dir)` runs the real live loop with all
81	    writes routed to rehearsal_dir; `exam_fn(recorded_day)` runs the exam engine. Both return a list of trade
82	    dicts (fields in _FIELDS + stock/instrument). Returns the verdict record and writes docs/reports/twin-<day>.md.
83	
84	    SAFETY: the live ledgers are hashed before and after; any change makes the verdict FAILED-SAFETY regardless
85	    of the trade comparison."""
86	    import tempfile
87	    rehearsal_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="twin_rehearsal_")
88	    before = hash_ledgers()
89	    live_trades = live_loop_fn(recorded_day, rehearsal_dir)
90	    exam_trades = exam_fn(recorded_day)
91	    after = hash_ledgers()
92	    touched = changed(before, after)
93	    if touched:
94	        rec = {"day": recorded_day, "verdict": "FAILED-SAFETY", "touched_live_ledgers": touched,
95	               "differences": [], "note": "the rehearsal changed a LIVE ledger — the run is void"}
96	    else:
97	        cmp = compare(live_trades, exam_trades)
98	        rec = {"day": recorded_day, "verdict": cmp["verdict"], "touched_live_ledgers": [],
99	               "differences": cmp["differences"],
100	               "counts": {"live": len(live_trades or []), "exam": len(exam_trades or [])}}
101	    _write_report(rec, live_trades, exam_trades, out or os.path.join(REPORTS, "twin-%s.md" % recorded_day))
102	    return rec
103	
104	
105	def _row(t):
106	    return "| " + " | ".join(str(t.get(f, "—")) for f in (("stock",) + _FIELDS)) + " |"
107	
108	
109	def _write_report(rec, live_trades, exam_trades, out):
110	    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out), exist_ok=True)
111	    head = ("# twin-%s — live loop vs exam, one recorded day\n\n"
112	            "**VERDICT: %s**  ·  live trades %s · exam trades %s  ·  written %s\n\n" % (
113	                rec["day"], rec["verdict"], (rec.get("counts") or {}).get("live", "—"),
114	                (rec.get("counts") or {}).get("exam", "—"), datetime.now(IST).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M IST")))
115	    if rec["verdict"] == "FAILED-SAFETY":
116	        head += ("The rehearsal changed a LIVE ledger — the run is VOID and this is a defect, not a mismatch:\n" +
117	                 "".join("- %s\n" % p for p in rec["touched_live_ledgers"]))
118	    elif rec["differences"]:
119	        head += "Differences (the exam is the reference; a difference is a defect in the live loop):\n\n" + \
120	                "\n".join(rec["differences"]) + "\n"
121	    else:
122	        head += "MATCH — every trade agrees on signal time, verdict/reason, fill, exit and P&L after charges.\n"
123	    hdr = "| " + " | ".join(("stock",) + _FIELDS) + " |\n|" + "---|" * (1 + len(_FIELDS))
124	    body = ("\n\n## Live loop (rehearsal)\n\n%s\n%s\n\n## Exam engine\n\n%s\n%s\n" % (
125	        hdr, "\n".join(_row(t) for t in (live_trades or [])) or "(none)",
126	        hdr, "\n".join(_row(t) for t in (exam_trades or [])) or "(none)"))
127	    with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
128	        f.write(head + body)
129	    return out
130	
131	
132	# The real runners — supplied at run time (after 15:45, memory-safe). They are deliberately NOT invoked here;
133	# calling one without the run-time wiring raises, so a stray call can never quietly run the heavy loop.
134	def real_live_loop(recorded_day, rehearsal_dir):
135	    raise NotImplementedError(
136	        "wire at run time (after 15:45): feed the recorded day through book20_runner/live_20d with ledger paths "
137	        "pointed at rehearsal_dir (never a reports/ path), and return the trades. The harness hashes the live "
138	        "ledgers around this call as the second safety net.")
139

142	    raise NotImplementedError(
143	        "wire at run time: run the exam engine (engine1_rebuild.paper.simulate_trade over the recorded day with "
144	        "the five live patterns) and return the trades.")

147	def _demo():
148	    import tempfile, json
149	    d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
150	    led = os.path.join(d, "BOOK20-LEDGER.csv"); open(led, "w").write("a,b\n1,2\n")
151	    trades = [{"stock": "UNITDSPR", "signal_time": "10:00", "verdict": "PLACED", "reason": "",
152	               "fill_price": 100.0, "exit_time": "15:20", "exit_reason": "target", "pnl_R": 1.5}]
153	    # 1) a clean rehearsal (touches nothing) + identical trade lists -> MATCH
154	    r = run("2026-09-22",
155	            live_loop_fn=lambda day, rd: list(trades),
156	            exam_fn=lambda day: list(trades),
157	            out=os.path.join(d, "twin_match.md"))
158	    # patch live_ledgers to our temp file for the safety check within this demo
159	    assert r["verdict"] == "MATCH", r
160	    # 2) a difference is caught and numbered
161	    other = [dict(trades[0], pnl_R=-1.0, exit_reason="stop")]
162	    c = compare(trades, other)
163	    assert c["verdict"] == "DIFF" and any("pnl_R" in x for x in c["differences"]), c
164	    # 3) DETECTIVE safety: a rehearsal that changes a live ledger is FAILED-SAFETY
165	    import sys
166	    M = sys.modules[__name__]                          # patch the RUNNING module (handles __main__ vs import)
167	    orig = M.live_ledgers
168	    M.live_ledgers = lambda: [led]
169	    try:
170	        def tamper(day, rd):
171	            open(led, "a").write("3,4\n")            # a rehearsal that (wrongly) writes a live ledger
172	            return list(trades)
173	        r2 = run("2026-09-22", live_loop_fn=tamper, exam_fn=lambda day: list(trades),
174	                 out=os.path.join(d, "twin_unsafe.md"))
175	        assert r2["verdict"] == "FAILED-SAFETY" and r2["touched_live_ledgers"] == [led], r2
176	    finally:
177	        M.live_ledgers = orig
178	    # 4) real runners never run silently
179	    for fn in (real_live_loop, real_exam):
180	        try:
181	            fn("2026-09-22", "x") if fn is real_live_loop else fn("2026-09-22")
182	            raise AssertionError("real runner should refuse without run-time wiring")
183	        except NotImplementedError:
184	            pass
185	    print("twin_run demo OK: MATCH, numbered DIFF, FAILED-SAFETY on a touched live ledger, real runners refuse.")

189	    import sys
190	    _demo() if "--demo" in sys.argv else print("twin_run: %d live ledgers guarded" % len(live_ledgers()))

29	def _real_order_verdict(flag_present):
30	    """The order-path decision for a queued signal. flag present -> HELD (fail-closed); else the two-key gate."""
31	    from engine1_rebuild import gate

42	def dry_run(asof, flag_present, status_0815=None, quotes=None,
43	            evaluate_fn=None, order_verdict_fn=None, line_0815_fn=None, log_path=None):
44	    """One pass. Returns {asof, flag_present, ready_line, queued, cards}. Writes the cards to the dry-run log."""
45	    evaluate_fn = evaluate_fn or _real_evaluate
46	    order_verdict_fn = order_verdict_fn or _real_order_verdict
47	    line_0815_fn = line_0815_fn or _real_0815
48	    ready = line_0815_fn(status_0815 or {})
49	    decisions = evaluate_fn(asof, quotes)
50	    queue = [d for d in decisions if d.get("verdict") == "QUEUE"]
51	    verdict = order_verdict_fn(flag_present)
52	    cards = [{"stock": d["stock"], "family": d["family"], "order": verdict} for d in queue]
53	    entry = {"stamp": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(), "asof": asof, "flag_present": flag_present,
54	             "ready_line": ready, "queued": len(queue), "cards": cards}
55	    with open(log_path or DRY_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:   # dry-run log ONLY — not Telegram, not the ledger
56	        f.write(json.dumps(entry) + "\n")
57	    return entry
58	
59	
60	def both_passes(asof, status_0815=None, quotes=None, **fns):
61	    """The two required passes. Returns (held_pass, placed_pass, ok) — ok iff flag-present HELD and flag-absent PLACED."""
62	    held = dry_run(asof, True, status_0815, quotes, **fns)
63	    placed = dry_run(asof, False, status_0815, quotes, **fns)
64	    ok = (all(c["order"] == "HELD" for c in held["cards"]) and held["cards"] != [] and
65	          all(c["order"] == "PLACED" for c in placed["cards"]) and placed["cards"] != [])
66	    return held, placed, ok

tool_result: 360	def run_once(asof=None, reader=None, write=True, quotes=None):
361	    """One poll of the single order path. Returns a readout. Writes an order ONLY when gate.order_ok holds
362	    for it on this poll; under the R-HOLD-AND-FIX hold that is never, so this records the would-be book."""
363	    now_iso = clock.iso()
364	    streak = _live_loss_streak()
365	    cost_frac = money.COST_STATUTORY_RT + 2 * money.half_spread()
366	    cands = _candidates(asof, reader)
367	    by_stock = {c["stock"]: c for c in cands}
368	    # desk rules as CALLERS (R-HOLD-AND-FIX 2): net same-stock fires, and pause on the measured book guard.
369	    fired_orders = [{"stock": c["stock"], "bucket": "daily", "direction": c["direction"], "size": 1.0,
370	                     "strength": abs(c["p_lb"]), "sentence": c["sentence"]} for c in cands]
371	    netted = {int(o["stock"]): o for o in desk.net_orders(fired_orders)} if fired_orders else {}
372	    # R-BOOK20-COMPLETE (3): book guard (measured worst daily DD) + position cap (measured), both fail-closed.
373	    open_now = _open_position_count()
374	    open_keys = _open_pattern_keys()                   # R-FIX-3E (1): (instrument, pattern) already holding a position
375	    block_all = ("*", "*") in open_keys                # fail-closed sentinel: unreadable ledger → no new fires
376	    pos_cap = _measured_position_cap()
377	    cap_paused = open_now >= pos_cap
378	    guard_paused, guard_reason = desk.book_guard(_today_loss_rupees(), _measured_worst_daily_dd())
379	    would_be, written = [], []
380	    for stock, c in list(by_stock.items()):
381	        if stock not in netted:                       # netting cancelled this stock's fires -> no order
382	            continue
383	        c["direction"] = "direct" if netted[stock]["direction"] in ("direct", "long") else "inverse"
384	        tm = _typical_move(c["daily"]["close"] if hasattr(c["daily"], "columns") else c["daily"])
385	        if tm is None:
386	            continue
387	        ok, required = live_20d.required_move_ok(c["p_lb"], cost_frac, tm)

394	        else:

396	        row = {"stock": c["stock"], "direction": c["direction"], "sentence": c["sentence"],
397	               "pattern": c.get("sentence", ""),      # R-FIX-3E (1): the open row carries its pattern for the re-fire key
398	               "required_move": round(required, 5), "typical_move": round(tm, 5),
399	               "moment_green": bool(m_ok), "horizon_days": H}
400	        would_be.append(row)
401	        # R-FIX-3E (1): NO RE-FIRE — never open a second position in a stock this pattern already holds.
402	        refire = block_all or (str(c["stock"]).strip(), str(c.get("sentence", "")).strip()) in open_keys
403	        allowed = (_exam_conform_proven() and (not guard_paused) and (not cap_paused) and (not refire)

407	        if write and allowed:

415	                                    _atr_of(c), H, sentence=c.get("sentence", ""), cost_frac=cost_frac)

418	                    written.append({**row, **pos})

420	            except Exception as e:

422	    if written:
423	        _append_orders(written)
424	    managed = _manage_open_positions(quotes)          # manage_position called per open position (📉 on exit)
425	    filled_this_poll = written

427	               "live_loss_streak": streak, "candidates": len(cands), "netted_orders": len(netted),

436	    try:

438	            json.dump(readout, f, indent=1, default=str)

## NB39 — unpark with isolation

**The ISOLATION LAW — recorded (CLAUDE.md §27).** The §24 freeze is replaced by isolation, so work unparks without endangering the money chain: **(a)** the Price Engine chain's files, launch path, and process list are off-limits to everything else; **(b)** every other engine is its own **diary-only** process (no orders) until it passes its own audit + smoke check; **(c)** others start only after the 08:15 READY line and only if measured free RAM ≥ the reopen floor + that process's own measured footprint, and are **killed first** under memory pressure (the chain never); **(d)** their failures are **quiet** (✅/⏱ + a diary/held log, never 🙋); **(e)** site work is front-end only. Root swap stays owner-gated.

105	def _short_reason(sentence):
106	    """A short plain phrase from a real signal sentence, e.g. 'when VWAP is in its lowest 20%'."""
107	    body = sentence.split(":", 1)[-1] if ":" in sentence else sentence
108	    body = body.split("→")[0].split("->")[0]         # cut at the arrow
109	    body = body.replace("daily,", "").replace("daily ", "").strip(" ,.")
110	    return (body[:60] + "…") if len(body) > 62 else body

tool_result: 362	def _stocks_data():
363	    """The Stocks page: every stock the desk watches, from the real screener (site/data/screener.json, 210
364	    rows). Real columns bind — name, volume (rvol), trend — and the readings the screener does not carry
365	    (RSI/MFI/price-range percentile/5-20d returns/gap) show an honest '—' rather than a fabricated
366	    number (§4.4). 'Signal today' is cross-referenced from the real calls (live.json), not invented; a
367	    stock is 'ready' when its screener row is not stale. The design's 30 procedurally-generated stocks are
368	    dropped."""
369	    import json
370	    def _load(path, key=None):
371	        try:
372	            d = json.load(open(os.path.join(REPO, path), encoding="utf-8"))
373	            return d.get(key) if key else d
374	        except Exception:
375	            return None
376	    rows_src = _load("site/data/screener.json", "rows") or []
377	    sig_stocks = {(c.get("stock") or "").upper() for c in (_load("site/data/live.json", "calls") or [])}
378	    DASH, GREY, GREEN = "—", "#9a9a93", "#5DCAA5"
379	    out = []
380	    for r in rows_src:
381	        sym = (r.get("symbol") or "").upper()
382	        sig = sym in sig_stocks
383	        rvol = r.get("rvol")
384	        out.append({
385	            "name": sym, "range": DASH, "rc": GREY,
386	            "mom": DASH, "calm": DASH, "flow": DASH,
387	            "vol": ("%.1fx" % rvol) if isinstance(rvol, (int, float)) else DASH,
388	            "gap": DASH,
389	            "met": "signal" if sig else DASH, "mc": GREEN if sig else GREY,
390	            "m5": DASH, "m20": DASH, "gapd": DASH,
391	            "trend": r.get("trend") or DASH, "r60": DASH,
392	            "sig": sig, "ready": not r.get("stale", False),
393	        })
394	    out.sort(key=lambda x: (not x["sig"], x["name"]))     # real signals first, then alphabetical
395	    ready_n = sum(1 for x in out if x["ready"])
396	    on, off, onFg, offFg = "#5DCAA5", "#161920", "#0f1115", "#b8b6ae"
397	    return {
398	        "rows": out, "count": len(out), "note": "signals first, then the rest of the watch list",
399	        "allBg": on, "allFg": onFg, "sigBg": off, "sigFg": offFg, "readyBg": off, "readyFg": offFg,
400	        "pickAll": "", "pickSig": "", "pickReady": "", "typeQuery": "",
401	        "_ready_n": ready_n, "_total": len(out),   # consumed by _fix_stocks, stripped before render
402	    }

tool_result: 195	def _data_data():
196	    """The Data page: one row per recorded stream, from the real stores (vitals._streams). Every field is a
197	    real value or empty — no sample number. The per-lane detail stays closed (open=False), so the sc-if detail
198	    fields are not required."""
199	    from scripts import vitals
200	    used_by = {"Order-book depth": "E1 (the moment light)", "Option chains": "E4 (options)",
201	               "NSE/BSE two-exchange": "E12 (two-exchange)", "Retail positioning": "E14 (positioning)"}
202	    JUDGE_DAYS = 20                       # a stream can be judged once it has ~20 recorded sessions (declared)
203	    lanes = []
204	    for name, s in (vitals._streams() or {}).items():
205	        rows = s.get("rows_10min")
206	        recording = bool(rows) or bool(s.get("last"))
207	        lanes.append({
208	            "name": name,
209	            "status": "Recording data" if recording else "No data today",
210	            "statusColor": "#5DCAA5" if recording else "#F09595",
211	            "rows": rows if rows is not None else "—",
212	            "today": rows if rows is not None else "—",
213	            "last": s.get("last") or "—",
214	            "days": 0, "countdown": JUDGE_DAYS,          # days recorded / days until it can be judged
215	            "strip": "", "stripColor": "#23262d", "sample": "", "usedBy": used_by.get(name, "the desk"),
216	            "open": False, "toggle": "",
217	        })
218	    return {"lanes": lanes}

def _scrub(page):
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", page)                 # visible text only
    hits = []
    for pat in BANNED:

            hits.append(m)
    return sorted(set(hits))

43	def _pattern_cards():
44	    """Score cards from the 937-call book, grouped by FAMILY (the ruler). Per family: cases, win rate,
45	    confidence-adjusted win rate (Wilson LB across its cases), average move after costs (mean net R), and the
46	    stage. Every number computed from the blotter + the certified registry — nothing typed."""
47	    fams = {}
48	    if os.path.exists(BLOTTER):
49	        for r in csv.DictReader(open(BLOTTER, encoding="utf-8")):
50	            f = str(r.get("family", "")).split("+")[0]
51	            d = fams.setdefault(f, {"cases": 0, "wins": 0, "sumR": 0.0})
52	            d["cases"] += 1
53	            try:
54	                rr = float(r.get("result_R") or 0)
55	            except Exception:
56	                rr = 0.0
57	            d["sumR"] += rr
58	            d["wins"] += 1 if str(r.get("correct")).strip().lower() == "true" else 0
59	    rows = []
60	    for f, d in sorted(fams.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1]["cases"]):
61	        n, w = d["cases"], d["wins"]
62	        wr = w / n if n else 0
63	        lb = _wilson_lb(w, n)
64	        avg = d["sumR"] / n if n else 0
65	        rows.append((_RULER.get(f, f), n, wr, lb, avg))
66	    return rows
67	
68	
69	def _wilson_lb(w, n, z=1.96):
70	    if n == 0:
71	        return 0.0
72	    p = w / n
73	    denom = 1 + z * z / n
74	    centre = p + z * z / (2 * n)
75	    margin = z * ((p * (1 - p) / n + z * z / (4 * n * n)) ** 0.5)
76	    return max(0.0, (centre - margin) / denom)
77	
78	
79	def _funnel():
80	    """The validation funnel counts, from disk, each captioned in plain words. Certified = the certified
81	    per-stock registries; live paper = the required-move book's calls. The exam-passed count is the phenomena
82	    headline the site already publishes; the intermediate stages are labelled from the pipeline."""
83	    certified = 0
84	    for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(PATTERN_STORE, "registry_*.parquet")):
85	        try:
86	            import pyarrow.parquet as pq
87	            certified += pq.ParquetFile(p).metadata.num_rows
88	        except Exception:
89	            pass
90	    live = max(0, sum(1 for _ in open(BLOTTER, encoding="utf-8")) - 1) if os.path.exists(BLOTTER) else 0
91	    return [
92	        ("Found", "2,274,419", "every behaviour the search turned up across the 210 stocks and all timeframes"),
93	        ("Sealed-months exam", "{:,}".format(certified), "kept only what still held on months the search never saw"),
94	        ("Walk-forward", "keeps updating", "re-scored on every live day — the number moves as real days arrive"),
95	        ("Luck correction", "applied", "kept only what beats what luck alone could look like (multiple-testing)"),
96	        ("Certified", "{:,}".format(certified), "the patterns that survived every check above"),
97	        ("Live paper", "{:,}".format(live), "the calls actually taken on paper at the 20-day horizon"),
98	    ]
99

101	def _calls_and_excel():
102	    try:
103	        from scripts import deliverables_page as DP
104	        DP.deliverables_section()          # writes site/deliverables.xlsx
105	        back = DP.backtest_calls()
106	        head = "".join("<th>%s</th>" % c for c in ("date", "stock", "buy/sell", "why", "size ₹", "result ₹", "status"))
107	        body = "".join("<tr>" + "".join("<td>%s</td>" % html.escape(str(r.get(c, ""))) for c in
108	                       ("date", "stock", "buy/sell", "why", "size ₹", "result ₹", "status")) + "</tr>" for r in back[:300])
109	        return ("<a class='dlbtn' href='deliverables.xlsx' download style='display:inline-block;background:#137a3f;"
110	                "color:#fff;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:8px;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600'>"

113	                "</tr>" + body + "</table><p class='muted'>Showing 300 of " + str(len(back)) +
114	                " — the Excel has them all.</p>")

116	        return "<p class='muted'>Calls unavailable.</p>"

119	def build_e1():
120	    from engine1_rebuild import paper_account as ACC
121	    a, paper_line, opens = _account()
122	    cards = "".join("<tr><td>%s</td><td>%d</td><td>%.0f%%</td><td>%.0f%%</td><td>%s%.2fR</td><td>certified · live</td></tr>"
123	                    % (html.escape(name), n, wr * 100, lb * 100, "+" if avg >= 0 else "", avg)
124	                    for name, n, wr, lb, avg in _pattern_cards())
125	    funnel = "".join("<tr><td><b>%s</b></td><td>%s</td><td class='muted'>%s</td></tr>"
126	                     % (html.escape(s), html.escape(v), html.escape(cap)) for s, v, cap in _funnel())
127	    acct = ("<div class='cards'><div class='cell'>equity<b>" + ACC.rupees(a["equity"]) + "</b></div>"
128	            "<div class='cell'>open trades<b>" + str(a.get("open_count", 0)) + "</b></div>"
129	            "<div class='cell'>paper trades<b>" + html.escape(paper_line) + "</b></div></div>" +
130	            (("<table><tr><th>stock</th><th>side</th><th>entry</th><th>P&L</th></tr>" + opens + "</table>")
131	             if opens else "<p class='muted'>No open paper trades right now.</p>"))
132	    grid = ("<p class='muted'>The full 210-stock × every-metric grid (today's values, lowest-20% / highest-20% "
133	            "coloured, tap a stock to open it) wires in <b>after your yes</b> — the one piece this page waits on.</p>")
134	    rows = ("<p class='muted'>Each metric's value → the rule it feeds → the patterns it meets → the signal if "
135	            "fired. Opens from the grid; wired with it.</p>")
136	    patterns = ("<table><tr><th>Family (the ruler)</th><th>Past cases</th><th>Win rate</th>"
137	                "<th>Confidence-adjusted</th><th>Avg move after costs</th><th>Stage</th></tr>" + cards + "</table>"
138	                "<p class='muted'>Grouped by the ruler here; every individual pattern is in the Excel below.</p>")
139	    funnel_html = "<table><tr><th>Stage</th><th>Count</th><th>What it means</th></tr>" + funnel + "</table>"
140	    timeline = ("<p class='muted'>This stock's story over the last 30 days — the metric, the signal day, the "
141	                "order, the exit. Opens per stock from the grid; wired with it.</p>")
142	    sections = [
143	        ("sec-account", "1 · Account", acct),
144	        ("sec-grid", "2 · The grid — 210 stocks × every metric", grid),
145	        ("sec-rows", "3 · A stock's rows", rows),
146	        ("sec-patterns", "4 · Patterns — score cards", patterns),
147	        ("sec-funnel", "5 · Validation funnel", funnel_html),
148	        ("sec-timeline", "6 · Timeline", timeline),
149	        ("sec-calls", "7 · Calls", _calls_and_excel()),
150	    ]
151	    doc = _shell("Engine 1 — deep dive", "Engine 1 — patterns in a stock's own price",
152	                 countdown_to_verdict("E1"), sections)
153	    out = os.path.join(REPO, "site", "engine-e1.html")
154	    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out), exist_ok=True)
155	    with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
156	        f.write(doc)
157	    return {"out": out, "families": len(_pattern_cards())}

160	def _shell(title, subtitle, countdown, sections):
161	    """R-DEEP-DIVE amendment: the engine pages use the site's own DARK terminal theme (app.css) + the same
162	    header. Every section is a remembered collapse/expand (<details> + localStorage), with Collapse-all /
163	    Expand-all. `sections` is a list of (id, heading, html)."""
164	    secs = "".join(
165	        "<details class='dd-sec' id='%s' open><summary>%s</summary><div class='dd-body'>%s</div></details>"
166	        % (sid, html.escape(head), body) for sid, head, body in sections)
167	    css = (".dd-sec{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;background:var(--panel);margin:10px 0;"
168	           "overflow:hidden}.dd-sec>summary{cursor:pointer;padding:10px 14px;font-weight:600;font-size:15px;"
169	           "list-style:none}.dd-sec>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}"
170	           ".dd-sec>summary::before{content:'\\25be';margin-right:8px;color:var(--ink-3)}"
171	           ".dd-sec:not([open])>summary::before{content:'\\25b8'}.dd-body{padding:2px 14px 14px}"
172	           "main{max-width:1040px;margin:0 auto;padding:14px 16px 60px}h1{font-size:20px;margin:6px 0}"
173	           "td,th{border:1px solid var(--line);padding:6px 9px;text-align:left}th{background:var(--panel-2)}"
174	           "table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:13px;margin-top:6px}a{color:#4cc57e}"
175	           ".cards{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:8px 0}.cell{border:1px solid var(--line);"
176	           "border-radius:10px;padding:10px 14px;background:var(--panel-2)}.cell b{display:block;font-size:18px}"
177	           ".dd-bar{margin:8px 0}.dd-bar button{background:transparent;color:var(--ink-2);border:1px solid "
178	           "var(--line);border-radius:999px;padding:3px 12px;cursor:pointer;margin-right:6px;font-size:12px}"
179	           ".dd-cd{background:var(--panel-2);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:8px 13px;"
180	           "margin:8px 0;color:var(--ink)}")
181	    script = ("<script>(function(){var K='dd:'+location.pathname;"
182	              "document.querySelectorAll('details.dd-sec').forEach(function(d){"
183	              "try{var v=localStorage.getItem(K+':'+d.id);if(v==='0')d.open=false;if(v==='1')d.open=true;}catch(e){}"
184	              "d.addEventListener('toggle',function(){try{localStorage.setItem(K+':'+d.id,d.open?'1':'0');}catch(e){}});});"
185	              "function all(o){document.querySelectorAll('details.dd-sec').forEach(function(d){d.open=o;"
186	              "try{localStorage.setItem(K+':'+d.id,o?'1':'0');}catch(e){}});}"
187	              "var c=document.getElementById('dd-collapse'),e=document.getElementById('dd-expand');"
188	              "if(c)c.onclick=function(){all(false);};if(e)e.onclick=function(){all(true);};})();</script>")
189	    header = ("<header class='top'><div class='brand'><span class='logo'>E1</span>"
190	              "<span class='brand-name'>Terminal</span><span class='pill paper'>PAPER ONLY</span></div>"
191	              "<div class='status'><a href='index.html#engines' class='pill'>&larr; back to the desk</a></div></header>")
192	    return ("<!doctype html><html lang='en'><head><meta charset='utf-8'>"
193	            "<meta name='viewport' content='width=device-width,initial-scale=1'><title>" + title + "</title>"
194	            "<link rel='stylesheet' href='app.css'><style>" + css + "</style></head><body>" + header +
195	            "<main><h1>" + subtitle + "</h1>"
196	            "<div class='dd-cd'>" + countdown + "</div>"
197	            "<div class='dd-bar'><button id='dd-collapse'>Collapse all</button>"
198	            "<button id='dd-expand'>Expand all</button></div>" + secs + "</main>" + script + "</body></html>")

200	
201	def countdown_to_verdict(engine="E1"):
202	    """A plain 'needs N more …' line, derived from the engine's own verdict rule + the ledgers — never typed.
203	    E1/E10/E11: the live-paper book validates on accumulated LIVE trades; count what's taken so far."""
204	    try:
205	        from engine1_rebuild import paper, money
206	        live = 0
207	        try:
208	            led = paper.read_ledger()
209	            if led is not None and len(led):
210	                if "trade_id" in led.columns:
211	                    led = led.drop_duplicates("trade_id", keep="last")
212	                live = int((led["trade_id"].astype(str).str.startswith("BOOK20-") &
213	                            led["status"].isin(["closed", "exited", "stopped", "timed-out"])).sum())
214	        except Exception:
215	            pass
216	        unlocked = len(money.unlocked_stocks())
217	        if live == 0:
218	            return ("Needs live paper trades to validate — 0 taken so far; the book is unheld from today, so "
219	                    "it starts counting tomorrow. Depth: %d of 210 stocks have the 20 readings a green light "
220	                    "needs." % unlocked)
221	        return ("%d live paper trades taken; the live edge becomes measurable as more arrive (depth: %d of 210 "
222	                "stocks ready)." % (live, unlocked))
223	    except Exception:
224	        return "Countdown unavailable."
225	
226	
227	def build():
228	    return build_e1()

235	                 "Download Excel", "after your yes"):
236	        assert must in doc, must
237	    print("engine_page demo OK: E1 deep-dive built (%d family score cards); grid/section-3/6 placeheld "
238	          "pending approval." % r["families"])

242	    _demo() if "--demo" in sys.argv else print(build())
